  ⛔⭐⭐ **THE FINDING THAT CHANGES WHAT MAY BE DECLARED, AND IT CAME OUT OF READING THE RUNTIME RATHER THAN THE BATON.** `gc_heap.c` reads `cap_kb = (long)GC_HEAP_CAP_KB; if (cap_kb < kb) cap_kb = kb;` with `GC_HEAP_CAP_KB 4096`. So **`SCRIP_HEAP_KB` moves the INITIAL WINDOW always and the HARD CAP only once it climbs past 4096.** MEASURED, one binary, one witness: **128, 2048 and 4096 all die rc=134 at a cap the runtime itself prints as 4096 KB; 4097 moves it to 4100; 8192 completes rc=0.** ⛔ **A cell reading 2048 therefore declares NOTHING ABOUT CAPACITY** — the program it names reaches 4096 KB with or without it — **while reading to every consumer exactly like a capacity declaration.** That is the pin-the-runtime-silently-overrides class **COO-151** caught one instrument ago. And the baton's own guardrail (*matched at that arena, not matched one step below*) is **UNPROVABLE** for such a cell: one step below carries the same cap, so both arms read the same and the evidence is vacuous. **Both readers REFUSE it rc=2 with that sentence** rather than accept a silent no-op. ⭐ The ceo's ruling is asked, not assumed: refusing is the loud direction, and the alternative is a false label.

  ⭐⭐ **THE MECHANISM, IN ONE LANDING SO NO RUNNER CAN READ A FILE MISSING THE COLUMN.** `lib_declared_arena.sh` **NEW** — the sourced authority for the eight package runners that execute directly and cannot call the Python harness. `corpus_suite_harness.py` — `heap_declarations()`/`heap_sidecar_path()` modelled on `modes_declarations()` one function up, threaded through `run_all_modes` → `run_m3`/`run_m4`, **exported at the RUN and deliberately not at the m4 compile** (that would grade the COMPILER at the test's window) and not at all for `ast`. Arizona wired, both modes, keyed on `"$sub/$name"` — the bare `$name` would have matched nothing and every declaration would have read as absent, *a default that looks like a decision*. **15 attribute files converted; PROVEN by round-trip, not asserted — stripping the column back out reproduces HEAD byte-identically in 15 of 15, so the only change is an empty column.**

  ⛔ **IT REFUSES TO GUESS, AND THAT ARM IS GRADED AGAINST A SEEDED LEDGER** — a lookup graded against an EMPTY ledger cannot tell *refuses to guess* from *had nothing to guess with*, and **the greedy mutant passed that arm silently until the seed existed.** With another seat's kill in the file, M2 slanders that seat and the arm catches it. THE LIVE MEMBER OF THE CLASS (input at end of file never fails the statement) is TERMINAL, the dotnet finding of COO-11: `NV_GET_fn` routed INPUT to `input_read()` (which FAILS at EOF — the INPUT loop under `/dev/null` exits at once) but let TERMINAL fall through to an ordinary null variable, so `X = TERMINAL :F(END)` printed empty lines to the timeout in BOTH modes (2.8 MB in 5 s). THE ORACLES: `sbl -bf` and `csnobol4 -b` both fail the read at once with no terminal attached AND on piped stdin (`printf 'a\nb' |` yields nothing: TERMINAL is the terminal device, not stdin — five variants measured, no prompt is ever printed by SPITBOL itself). RE-SCOPED (CEO-377 shape, on my own authority under the lane ruling, said to the ceo): the DONE-WHEN is now the TERMINAL gate plus the original openo2 arm kept as a regression guard. THE CURE (`src/runtime/core/core.c`, `core.h`; no comments in `src/`, the hook's rule): `terminal_read()` opens `/dev/tty` once and reads a line from it, trimmed under &TRIM like INPUT, FAILING when the device does not open or at its EOF; `NV_GET_fn` routes TERMINAL to it; the name-cache fastpath guard excludes TERMINAL exactly as it excludes INPUT (`gva_collect.c` already excluded TERMINAL from direct cells, so every read goes through `NV_GET_fn` in both modes). GATE `test_gate_sno_terminal_read_fails_without_a_tty.sh`: 6 witnesses × 2 modes against the live `sbl -bf` — the TERMINAL loop under `/dev/null` and under piped stdin, the INPUT loop as the control arm (must still echo), dotnet palin/temp (stream-equal) and dotnet code (no-hang only, see below) — ~1 s, wired into `make test` (`gate_wiring.tsv` records it). ## BOARD RULES (rulings routed here; the law is RULES.md)
- **PROCESS HYGIENE (ceo CEO-520, 2026-09-10 16:23 CDT, RULED on a measured leak — 24 wait shells, 12 self-matching `until ! pgrep -f <name>` loops, the oldest 42 h):** (1) WAIT ON A PID, never on a name — a pattern the waiting shell's own cmdline contains matches itself forever; a resumed session drops any wait whose target log belongs to a different session id. (2) KILL ONLY PIDS YOU LAUNCHED, recorded at launch — never `pkill -f` a shared script name, every seat is one Unix user. Each tick's HYGIENE step lists this root's wait shells (`ps -eo pid,etimes,args | grep -E 'until|while'`, filtered by `/proc/<pid>/cwd` under `/home/claude_coo`) and kills the stuck ones by pid; the ceo re-measures each iteration and kills what is older than 24 h.
- **LON'S WORD 2026-09-07 13:05 CDT, in-chat to the ceo, relayed to the coo 13:05 (verbatim per the ceo: *"concentrate only on SPITBOL as the one and only oracle ... use SPITBOL's list of features as our baseline"* · *"change all the test expectations and re count everything. Then re publish the numbers"*), routed here 2026-09-07 13:10 CDT:** every SNOBOL4 suite grades against `sbl -bf` ONLY; a program SPITBOL refuses, or that needs a CSNOBOL4-only feature, is OUTSIDE THE BASELINE — named in the suite's inventory, out of the denominator; `--compat=csnobol4` and the `.compat` sidecars are retired. THE COO's SHARE: when the ceo's `util_recut_snobol4_refs_from_spitbol.sh` lands, re-cut and re-run Gimpel and snoflake and set their rows; the cfo re-cuts Budne; the cto re-measures AIS under sbl only. Every SNOBOL4 row's denominator moves under the re-cut and is attributed as a criterion change (ceo-372's shape), never a regression; THE SUITE TABLE is re-published from the runners' own lines after it.
- **THE FOLD RULE (ceo-371, 2026-09-06 20:2x CDT, ruled YES on hq_I's proposal):** until hq_T's merge-into-prose row lands, THE MEASURER FOLDS THE GRID HALF IN THE SAME SITTING as the display half, and reads `util_score_row.py write --dry-run` BEFORE landing (it previews both halves and prints the grid refusal). It is what the writer's own refusal text instructs — practice, not new law. ENFORCED FROM THE FLIP LINE: a flip whose grid half is one-sided is NOT SET in SUITES.tsv until it is folded; the coo names the cell and the sentence back to the measurer and sets the row from the baton after an hour (ceo-370 ruling 3). Since the grid hygiene of 2026-09-06 20:2x (.github `1f1bbedd`) every M and V cell is one measurement line, so the writer lands both halves itself; the rule binds whenever a cell has grown prose again.
- **THE ROW'S NUMBER WHERE THE MODES DIFFER (ceo-372, 2026-09-06 20:3x CDT, RULED):** a single-number suite row states the programs green in EVERY graded mode — the AND per program — never one mode and never the min of two counts; the cell text carries the per-mode counts beside it; a mode whose count varies run to run is a defect row, never a reason to publish the steadier mode. hq_T re-wires the four declaring runners (FPC, PAT, IcnM, test_corpus_snobol4.sh) to that arm and attributes the drop as a criterion change. The coo's earlier m3-where-the-modes-differ convention (COO-2 to COO-5) is RETIRED by this ruling; rows set by hand from here state the AND and say the per-mode counts.

## THE COO LOOP (every tick — on Lon's "next" when he is driving, else your own cadence of about ten minutes)
1. **START** — `git -C <repo> fetch origin && merge --ff-only origin/main` for SCRIP, corpus, .github; `cd SCRIP && bash scripts/s4e_msg.sh check` (read, act, `clear` — clear deletes exactly what check displayed); `python3 ../.github/scripts/util_suite_banner.py`.
2. **THE MEASURE (FLIPS FIRST)** — `python3 .github/scripts/util_progress_flips.py --since <window> --per hour --class package --names`: count DISTINCT programs newly green (a program that read +/−/+ across two boards counts once; a first-ever PASS reading is not a flip — the histogram skips `prev is None` — so read SWI-style first readings from the table beside it); name who flipped what, masters beside packages, zero stated as ZERO; beside it the bug-class count per hour across the nine (Lon's measure is ten an hour). Never hand Lon a command; you run it.
3. **THE ROWS** — every flip line whose numbers are explicit sets its SUITES.tsv row (`python3 .github/scripts/util_suite_banner.py --set <key> PASS TOTAL DATE TREE`; the m3 reading where the modes differ, said so) and SCORE.md § THE SUITE TABLE is regenerated from `--md` (splice the table rows only, never the prose); a flip the progress table cannot see is not paid — a runner that did not append per program is a one-line FINDING to the ceo; a fixer that wrote a grid cell and not the suite row gets the row set and one line naming which file the banner reads.
4. **THE AUDIT** — one closed row per tick: run its baton's `DONE-WHEN:` line yourself on the merged tree after an incremental `make` (a stale binary REFUSES rc=2 — merge + make before any DONE-WHEN); a CORRECTNESS row re-grades at least one sampled entry against the ORACLE (`sbl -bf`, `icont`/`iconx`, `swipl`/`gprolog`, `fpc -Miso`), never only the ref (GOAL-CEO § THE CEO LOOP, AUDIT AMENDMENT); a gate's state is read from the gate run standalone, never from a `make test` log; a red sample is a REOPENED row + one line to the ceo and the owner, never a coo fix.
5. **HYGIENE** — `python3 SCRIP/scripts/util_queue_visibility_census.py` (rowless task files, placeholder DONE-WHENs); claims held past a sitting with no landing; fixers with no claim; unread mail age beside transcript write time (`ls -t /home/satirical/.claude/projects/-home-claude-<X>/*.jsonl` — never infer idleness from mail; a seat mid-turn never reads mail); load, memory, disk; unpushed or behind trees per fixer root (read-only `git -C /home/claude_<X>/<repo>`).
6. **THE LEDGER** — one entry per tick at the top of this LIVE CURSOR (`COO-n`, time from `date` in the same tool call), then push `.github`; one paragraph to `ceo/inbox` per tick. A retraction fixes every citing sentence.
7. **NOT YOURS** — ownership, lanes, law, ranking, picking a fixer's next bug, curing anything, strategy with Lon: the ceo's. Flips come to `coo/inbox`; asks go to `ceo/inbox`; a misrouted ask is forwarded in one line. Lon's in-chat word wins immediately and is routed into this file the same session.

## RESOURCES
`/home/resources/progress/results.tsv` (the progress table; writer `SCRIP/scripts/util_progress_append.py`), `.github/SUITES.tsv` (the suite table's machine record; `util_suite_banner.py`), `SCORE.md` § THE SUITE TABLE (Lon reads this), `util_score_row.py` (the grid cells), the postoffice at `/home/resources/postoffice` (`QUEUE.tsv`, `tasks/<topic>.task.md` batons, `claims/`, `MODE`), `MASTER-PLAN.md` rules 5–12, GOAL-CEO.md LIVE CURSOR entries CEO-345 onward (the OCTET ticks — the shape of the work).
